============================================================
ARQUIVO: src/components/home/FaqAccordion.tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';
import { FaqItem } from './FaqItem';

export interface FaqDataEntry {
  q: string;
  a: string;
  itens?: readonly string[];
}

interface FaqAccordionProps {
  titulo?: string;
  faqs: readonly FaqDataEntry[];
}

function FaqExpandButton({ isExpanded, onToggle }: { isExpanded: boolean; onToggle: () => void }) {
  const btnClass =
    'flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[rgba(239,246,255,0.5)] border border-[#dbeafe] ' +
    'py-[10px] px-6 text-sm font-medium text-[#1e40af] transition-colors hover:bg-[#eff6ff] ' +
    'cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-yellow-400';
  const iconClass = `w-4 h-4 ml-2 transition-transform duration-300 ${isExpanded ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`;

  return (
    <div className="mt-8 flex justify-center">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onToggle}
        className={btnClass}
        aria-expanded={isExpanded}
        aria-controls="faq-accordion-list"
      >
        <span>{isExpanded ? 'Ver menos' : 'Ver mais perguntas'}</span>
        <ChevronDown className={iconClass} aria-hidden="true" />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function FaqList({
  faqs,
  openIndex,
  onSelect,
}: {
  faqs: readonly FaqDataEntry[];
  openIndex: number | null;
  onSelect: (index: number) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div id="faq-accordion-list">
      {faqs.map((faq, index) => (
        <FaqItem
          key={`faq-item-${index}`}
          id={index + 1}
          index={index}
          question={faq.q}
          answer={faq.a}
          itens={faq.itens}
          isOpen={openIndex === index}
          onClick={() => onSelect(index)}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function useFaqState(totalFaqs: number) {
  const [openIndex, setOpenIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);

  const showExpandBtn = totalFaqs > 3;

  const handleToggle = () => {
    if (isExpanded) setOpenIndex(null);
    setIsExpanded(!isExpanded);
  };

  const handleSelect = (idx: number) => {
    setOpenIndex(openIndex === idx ? null : idx);
  };

  return { openIndex, isExpanded, showExpandBtn, handleToggle, handleSelect };
}

export const FaqAccordion: React.FC<FaqAccordionProps> = ({
  titulo = 'Perguntas Frequentes',
  faqs = [],
}) => {
  const { openIndex, isExpanded, showExpandBtn, handleToggle, handleSelect } =
    useFaqState(faqs.length);

  const visibleFaqs = showExpandBtn && !isExpanded ? faqs.slice(0, 3) : faqs;

  const cardCls =
    'bg-[#ffffff] border border-[#f3f4f6] rounded-[16px] shadow-sm p-6 md:p-8 flex flex-col justify-between w-full';

  return (
    <section
      id="perguntas"
      aria-labelledby="faq-heading"
      className="w-full bg-[#f9fafb] py-16 md:py-24 min-h-[500px]"
    >
      <div className="crasp-page-container">
        <h2 id="faq-heading" className="text-xl font-bold text-[#111827] mb-6">
          {titulo}
        </h2>
        <div className={cardCls}>
          <FaqList faqs={visibleFaqs} openIndex={openIndex} onSelect={handleSelect} />
          {showExpandBtn && <FaqExpandButton isExpanded={isExpanded} onToggle={handleToggle} />}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

============================================================
ARQUIVO: src/components/home/FaqItem.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';

export interface FaqItemProps {
  id: number | string;
  index: number;
  question: string;
  answer: string;
  itens?: readonly string[];
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClick: () => void;
}

function FaqBulletList({ itens }: { itens: readonly string[] }) {
  return (
    <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2 list-disc list-inside text-sm text-[#4b5563] leading-[1.625] pl-1">
      {itens.map((item, idx) => (
        <li key={idx} className="marker:text-[#1e40af]">
          <span className="text-[#374151]">{item}</span>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}

function FaqChevronIcon({ isOpen }: { isOpen: boolean }) {
  const iconClass =
    'w-5 h-[20px] text-[#6b7280] group-hover:text-blue-800 transition-transform duration-300 ' +
    (isOpen ? 'rotate-180' : '');

  return (
    <div
      className={
        'w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#f9fafb] group-hover:bg-[#eff6ff] ' +
        'flex items-center justify-center shrink-0'
      }
    >
      <ChevronDown className={iconClass} aria-hidden="true" />
    </div>
  );
}

function FaqButtonLabel({ id, question }: { id: number | string; question: string }) {
  return (
    <span className="text-base font-semibold text-[#111827] pr-4 group-hover:text-blue-800 transition-colors">
      {id}. {question}
    </span>
  );
}

function FaqItemButton(props: {
  id: number | string;
  triggerId: string;
  panelId: string;
  question: string;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClick: () => void;
}) {
  const btnClass =
    'flex items-center justify-between w-full py-6 group text-left cursor-pointer ' +
    'focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-800 ' +
    'focus-visible:ring-offset-2 rounded-lg';

  return (
    <button
      id={props.triggerId}
      type="button"
      onClick={props.onClick}
      className={btnClass}
      aria-expanded={props.isOpen}
      aria-controls={props.panelId}
    >
      <FaqButtonLabel id={props.id} question={props.question} />
      <FaqChevronIcon isOpen={props.isOpen} />
    </button>
  );
}

function FaqAnswerBody({ answer, itens }: { answer: string; itens?: readonly string[] }) {
  return (
    <>
      <p className="text-sm text-[#4b5563] leading-[1.625] whitespace-pre-line">{answer}</p>
      {itens && itens.length > 0 && <FaqBulletList itens={itens} />}
    </>
  );
}

function FaqItemContent(props: {
  panelId: string;
  triggerId: string;
  isOpen: boolean;
  answer: string;
  itens?: readonly string[];
}) {
  const bodyClass =
    'transition-all duration-300 overflow-hidden ' +
    (props.isOpen ? 'max-h-[40rem] opacity-100 pb-6' : 'max-h-0 opacity-0');

  return (
    <div
      id={props.panelId}
      role="region"
      aria-labelledby={props.triggerId}
      aria-hidden={!props.isOpen}
      className={bodyClass}
    >
      <FaqAnswerBody answer={props.answer} itens={props.itens} />
    </div>
  );
}

export const FaqItem: React.FC<FaqItemProps> = ({ id, index, question, answer, itens, isOpen, onClick }) => {
  const safeValue = String(id).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '_') || 'item';
  const triggerId = `faq-trigger-${index}-${safeValue}`;
  const panelId = `faq-panel-${index}-${safeValue}`;

  return (
    <div className="border-b border-[#e5e7eb] last:border-b-0">
      <FaqItemButton
        id={id}
        triggerId={triggerId}
        panelId={panelId}
        question={question}
        isOpen={isOpen}
        onClick={onClick}
      />
      <FaqItemContent
        panelId={panelId}
        triggerId={triggerId}
        isOpen={isOpen}
        answer={answer}
        itens={itens}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

import { partners } from '../../constants/partners';
import type { Partner } from '../../constants/partners';

export function HomeMarqueeLogoItem({ partner }: { partner: Partner }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-center min-w-[120px]">
      <img
        src={partner.img}
        alt={partner.title}
        width={120}
        height={40}
        className={
          'max-h-8 md:max-h-10 max-w-[120px] object-contain transition-all ' +
          `duration-500 ${partner.scaleClass || ''}`
        }
        style={{ filter: partner.baseFilter || 'none' }}
        onMouseEnter={(e) => {
          if (partner.hoverFilter) e.currentTarget.style.filter = partner.hoverFilter;
        }}
        onMouseLeave={(e) => {
          if (partner.baseFilter) e.currentTarget.style.filter = partner.baseFilter;
        }}
        referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export function HomeLogosBar() {
  return (
    <div
      className={

      }
    >
      <div className="relative flex overflow-hidden mx-auto max-w-7xl">
        <div className="flex w-max animate-marquee">
          {[...Array(4)].map((_, setIdx) => (
            <div key={setIdx} className="flex items-center gap-12 md:gap-20 pr-12 md:pr-20">
              {partners.map((partner, idx) => (
                <HomeMarqueeLogoItem key={idx} partner={partner} />

export function FormErrorNotice({ error }: { error: string }) {
  if (!error) return null;
  return (
    <div
      role="alert"
      aria-live="assertive"
      className={
        'form-error-notice mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 ' +
        'text-xs rounded-lg font-medium'
      }
    >
      {error}
    </div>
  );
}

============================================================
ARQUIVO: src/components/interest/interestFormUtils.ts
import {
  registrarManifestacaoInteresse,
  DuplicateLeadError,
} from '../../api/leadsService';
import type { LeadRegistrationInput } from '../../types/leads';

export const inputClass =
  'border border-gray-300 text-gray-900 px-3.5 py-2.5 w-full outline-none rounded-lg ' +
  'focus:border-[#172554] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#172554] transition-all bg-gray-50 ' +
  'hover:bg-white focus:bg-white text-sm';

export const labelClass = 'block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-1';

export function formatPhone(val: string): string {
  let clean = val.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (clean.length > 11 && clean.startsWith('55')) {
    clean = clean.slice(2);
  }
  clean = clean.slice(0, 11);
  if (clean.length <= 2) return clean ? `(${clean}` : '';
  if (clean.length <= 6) return `(${clean.slice(0, 2)}) ${clean.slice(2)}`;
  if (clean.length <= 10) {
    return `(${clean.slice(0, 2)}) ${clean.slice(2, 6)}-${clean.slice(6)}`;
  }
  return `(${clean.slice(0, 2)}) ${clean.slice(2, 7)}-${clean.slice(7)}`;
}

export async function submitInterestPayload(
  payload: LeadRegistrationInput,
  onSuccess: (data: { nome: string; instituicao: string }) => void
): Promise<void> {
  await registrarManifestacaoInteresse(payload);
  onSuccess({ nome: payload.nome, instituicao: payload.instituicao });
}

export function handleInterestError(err: unknown): string {
  if (err instanceof DuplicateLeadError || (err as { code?: string })?.code === 'DUPLICATE') {
    return 'Esta manifestação já foi cadastrada anteriormente para este e-mail institucional.';
  }
  const msg = (err as Error)?.message || '';
  if (/failed to fetch|networkerror|load failed/i.test(msg)) {
    return 'Falha de conexão com o servidor. Verifique sua conexão com a internet e tente novamente.';
  }
  return msg || 'Erro ao enviar manifestação. Tente novamente em instantes.';
}

============================================================
ARQUIVO: src/components/interest/useInterestForm.ts
import { useState } from 'react';
import type { FormEvent } from 'react';
import { handleInterestError } from './interestFormUtils';
import { registrarManifestacaoInteresse } from '../../api/leadsService';

export interface InterestFields {
  nome: string;
  cargo: string;
  instituicao: string;
  campus: string;
  email: string;
  telefone: string;
  curso: string;
  previsao: string;
  observacoes: string;
}

const EMPTY_FIELDS: InterestFields = {
  nome: '',
  cargo: '',
  instituicao: '',
  campus: '',
  email: '',
  telefone: '',
  curso: '',
  previsao: '',
  observacoes: '',
};

export function isPhoneValid(telefone: string): boolean {
  const digits = telefone.replace(/\D/g, '');
  return digits.length >= 10 && digits.length <= 11;
}

export function isFormValid(f: InterestFields): boolean {
  return Boolean(
    f.nome.trim() &&
    f.email.trim() &&
    isPhoneValid(f.telefone) &&
    f.instituicao.trim() &&
    f.campus.trim() &&
    f.previsao.trim()
  );
}

export function buildPayload(f: InterestFields) {
  return {
    nome: f.nome.trim(),
    email: f.email.trim(),
    telefone: f.telefone.trim(),
    instituicao: f.instituicao.trim(),
    campus: f.campus.trim(),
    cargo: f.cargo,
    curso: f.curso,
    previsaoSemestre: f.previsao,
    observacoes: f.observacoes.trim(),
  };
}

export function getFormValidationError(f: InterestFields): string {
  if (isFormValid(f)) return '';
  if (f.telefone.trim() && !isPhoneValid(f.telefone)) {
    return 'Telefone deve conter DDD e 8 ou 9 dígitos.';
  }
  return 'Por favor, preencha todos os campos obrigatórios.';
}

export function useInterestForm(props: {
  onSuccess: (data: { nome: string; instituicao: string }) => void;
}) {
  const [f, setF] = useState<InterestFields>(EMPTY_FIELDS);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const update = (key: keyof InterestFields, val: string) => {
    setF((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: val }));
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const valError = getFormValidationError(f);
    if (valError) return setError(valError);

    try {
      setIsLoading(true);
      setError('');
      await registrarManifestacaoInteresse(buildPayload(f));
      props.onSuccess({ nome: f.nome, instituicao: f.instituicao });
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setError(handleInterestError(err));
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return { f, update, isLoading, error, handleSubmit };
}

function InfoIcon() {

    <svg
      xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
      width="16"
      height="16"
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="none"

      strokeWidth="2"

      strokeLinejoin="round"
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10" />
      <path d="M12 16v-4" />
      <path d="M12 8h.01" />
    </svg>
  );

function FooterLinkItem({ link }: { link: { name: string; href: string } }) {
  if (link.href.startsWith('http')) {
    return (
      <a
        href={link.href}
        target="_blank"
        rel="noopener noreferrer"
        className="text-sm text-[#dbeafe] hover:text-white transition-colors"
      >
        {link.name}
      </a>
    );
  }
  return (
    <Link to={link.href} className="text-sm text-[#dbeafe] hover:text-white transition-colors">
      {link.name}
    </Link>
  );
}

function LinksColumn({
  title,
  links,
}: {
  title: string;
  links: { name: string; href: string }[];
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <h4 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white mb-4">{title}</h4>
      <div className="flex flex-col space-y-3">
        {links.map((link, idx) => (
          <FooterLinkItem key={idx} link={link} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

}

ARQUIVO: src/components/layout/HeaderDesktopNav.tsx
import { Link, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { headerNavigation } from '../../constants/navigation';

export function HeaderDesktopNav() {
  const location = useLocation();

  return (
    <nav className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-1 lg:gap-3" aria-label="Navegação Principal">
      {headerNavigation.map((item) => {
        const isActive = location.pathname === item.path;
        const cls =
          'block rounded-full px-4 py-3 text-base font-normal transition-colors cursor-pointer no-underline ' +
          'focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#1e40af] ' +
          (isActive ? 'bg-[#f9fafb] text-[#1e40af]' : 'text-[#374151] hover:bg-[#f9fafb] hover:text-[#1e40af]');

        return item.path !== '#' ? (
          <Link
            key={item.name}
            to={item.path}
            aria-current={isActive ? 'page' : undefined}
            className={cls}
          >
            {item.name}
          </Link>
        ) : (
          <button key={item.name} type="button" className={cls}>
            {item.name}
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </nav>
  );
}

ARQUIVO: src/components/layout/HeaderMobileMenu.tsx
import { Link, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { headerNavigation } from '../../constants/navigation';

interface HeaderMobileMenuProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function MobileMenuItem({
  item,
  onClose,
}: {
  item: { name: string; path: string };
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const location = useLocation();
  const isActive = location.pathname === item.path;
  const cls =
    'block rounded-full px-4 py-2.5 text-base font-normal transition-colors cursor-pointer no-underline ' +
    'focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#1e40af] ' +
    (isActive ? 'bg-[#f9fafb] text-[#1e40af]' : 'text-[#374151] hover:bg-[#f9fafb] hover:text-[#1e40af]');

  if (item.path !== '#') {
    return (
      <Link
        to={item.path}
        className={cls}
        aria-current={isActive ? 'page' : undefined}
        onClick={onClose}
      >
        {item.name}
      </Link>
    );
  }
  return (
    <button type="button" className={cls} onClick={onClose}>
      {item.name}
    </button>
  );
}

export function HeaderMobileMenu({ isOpen, onClose }: HeaderMobileMenuProps) {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <nav
      id="mobile-navigation-menu"
      className="md:hidden border-t border-[#f3f4f6] bg-white py-3 px-4 shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.05)]"
      aria-label="Menu Mobile"
    >
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        {headerNavigation.map((item) => (
          <MobileMenuItem key={item.name} item={item} onClose={onClose} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}

ARQUIVO: src/components/layout/PrivacyDialog.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { X, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react';

interface PrivacyDialogProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  triggerRef: React.RefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null>;
}

function PrivacyDialogHeader({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 mb-4">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-blue-100 text-[#1e40af] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
          <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5" aria-hidden="true" />
        </div>
        <h2 id="privacy-dialog-title" className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900 leading-tight">
          Diretrizes de Governança e Privacidade
        </h2>
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onClose}
        aria-label="Fechar diálogo de governança e privacidade"
        className={
          'p-2 rounded-lg text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors ' +
          'focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-600 cursor-pointer'
        }
      >
        <X className="w-5 h-5" aria-hidden="true" />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function PrivacyDialogDescription() {
  return (
    <div id="privacy-dialog-description" className="space-y-4 text-sm text-gray-600 leading-relaxed">
      <p>
        As preferências de tamanho de fonte e alto contraste deste portal são persistidas localmente
        no seu navegador para assegurar conforto visual e uma experiência inclusiva e personalizada.
      </p>
      <p>
        Este armazenamento local tem a finalidade exclusiva de preservar a usabilidade e as configurações de
        acessibilidade selecionadas pelo usuário durante sua navegação neste dispositivo.
      </p>
      <p>
        Para informações institucionais ou requisições de titulares, consulte os canais oficiais do Conselho
        Regional de Administração de São Paulo disponíveis no rodapé deste portal.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

function PrivacyDialogActions({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onClose}
        className={
          'px-5 py-2.5 bg-[#172554] text-white rounded-lg font-semibold text-sm hover:bg-blue-900 ' +
          'transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 ' +
          'focus-visible:ring-blue-600'
        }
      >
        Entendido
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function useNativeDialog(
  isOpen: boolean,
  onClose: () => void,
  triggerRef: React.RefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null>
) {
  const dialogRef = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const dialog = dialogRef.current;
    if (!dialog) return;
    if (isOpen && !dialog.open) dialog.showModal();
    if (!isOpen && dialog.open) dialog.close();
  }, [isOpen]);

  const handleClose = () => {
    if (dialogRef.current?.open) dialogRef.current.close();
    onClose();
    triggerRef.current?.focus();
  };

  const handleCancel = (e: React.SyntheticEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    handleClose();
  };

  return { dialogRef, handleClose, handleCancel };
}

export function PrivacyDialog({ isOpen, onClose, triggerRef }: PrivacyDialogProps) {
  const { dialogRef, handleClose, handleCancel } = useNativeDialog(isOpen, onClose, triggerRef);

  return (
    <dialog
      ref={dialogRef}
      onCancel={handleCancel}
      aria-labelledby="privacy-dialog-title"
      aria-describedby="privacy-dialog-description"
      className={
        'backdrop:bg-black/60 rounded-2xl p-6 sm:p-8 max-w-lg w-full bg-white shadow-2xl ' +
        'border border-gray-200 text-gray-800 m-auto'
      }
    >
      <PrivacyDialogHeader onClose={handleClose} />
      <PrivacyDialogDescription />
      <PrivacyDialogActions onClose={handleClose} />
    </dialog>
  );
}

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { Outlet, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { TopBar } from './TopBar';
import { Header } from './Header';

const PAGE_TITLES: Record<string, string> = {
  '/': 'Início | Componente Curricular de ASG e ODS | CRA-SP',

  '/registro-interesse': 'Manifestação de Interesse | CRA-SP',
};

export const PublicLayout = () => {
  const location = useLocation();

  useEffect(() => {
    document.title = PAGE_TITLES[location.pathname] || 'Componente Curricular de ASG e ODS | CRA-SP';
    if (location.hash) {
      const id = location.hash.replace('#', '');
      setTimeout(() => {
        document.getElementById(id)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
      }, 100);
    } else {
      window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    }
  }, [location.pathname, location.hash, location.key]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen w-full overflow-x-hidden bg-white text-gray-900 flex flex-col">
      <TopBar />
      <Header />
      <main className="flex-1 flex flex-col">
        <Outlet />
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';

const BASE_BTN_CLASS =
  'topbar-auth-btn bg-yellow-500 text-blue-900 rounded-full py-2 min-w-[134px] sm:min-w-[154px] ' +

  'focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-yellow-300 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 ' +

export function TopBarInnerContainer({
  acc,
  privacyButtonRef,
  onOpenPrivacy,
}: {
  acc: ReturnType<typeof useAccessibilitySync>;
  privacyButtonRef: React.RefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null>;
  onOpenPrivacy: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className={
        'max-w-[1152px] mx-auto py-2 px-3 sm:px-4 flex flex-wrap ' +
        'justify-between items-center gap-2 text-sm'
      }
    >
      <TopBarAccessibility
        fontSizeScale={acc.fontSizeScale}
        isHighContrast={acc.isHighContrast}
        onToggleHighContrast={acc.toggleHighContrast}
        onIncreaseFontSize={acc.increaseFontSize}
        onDecreaseFontSize={acc.decreaseFontSize}
        onResetFontSize={acc.resetFontSize}
        onPrivacyClick={onOpenPrivacy}
        privacyButtonRef={privacyButtonRef}
      />
      <TopBarRightSection />
    </div>
  );
}

export function TopBarRegion(props: {
  acc: ReturnType<typeof useAccessibilitySync>;
  privacyButtonRef: React.RefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null>;
  onOpenPrivacy: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      role="region"
      aria-label="Barra de Acessibilidade e Identificação Institucional"
      className="w-full bg-blue-900 border-b border-blue-800 text-blue-100"
    >
      <TopBarInnerContainer
        acc={props.acc}
        privacyButtonRef={props.privacyButtonRef}
        onOpenPrivacy={props.onOpenPrivacy}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export const TopBar: React.FC = () => {
  const [isPrivacyOpen, setIsPrivacyOpen] = useState(false);
  const privacyButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement | null>(null);
  const acc = useAccessibilitySync();

  return (
    <>
      <TopBarRegion
        acc={acc}
        privacyButtonRef={privacyButtonRef}
        onOpenPrivacy={() => setIsPrivacyOpen(true)}
      />
      <PrivacyDialog
        isOpen={isPrivacyOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsPrivacyOpen(false)}
        triggerRef={privacyButtonRef}
      />
    </>
  );
};

ARQUIVO: src/components/layout/TopBarAccessibility.tsx
import { forwardRef, type RefObject } from 'react';
import { Cookie } from 'lucide-react';
import { TopBarFontSizeButtons } from './TopBarFontSizeButtons';

interface TopBarAccessibilityProps {
  fontSizeScale: number;
  isHighContrast: boolean;
  onToggleHighContrast: () => void;
  onIncreaseFontSize: () => void;
  onDecreaseFontSize: () => void;
  onResetFontSize?: () => void;
  onPrivacyClick: () => void;
  privacyButtonRef: RefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null>;
}

export function ContrastIcon() {
  return (
    <svg
      xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
      width="16"
      height="16"
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth="2.2"
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      className="crasp-a11y-icon w-3.5 h-3.5 sm:w-4 sm:h-4"
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10" />
      <path d="M12 2a10 10 0 0 1 10 10 10 10 0 0 1-10 10V2z" />
    </svg>
  );
}

export function ContrastButton({
  isHighContrast,
  onToggle,
}: {
  isHighContrast: boolean;
  onToggle: () => void;
}) {
  const base =
    'crasp-a11y-circle-btn crasp-a11y-btn-contrast w-7 h-7 sm:w-[1.875rem] sm:h-[1.875rem] rounded-full ' +
    'inline-flex items-center justify-center text-inherit transition-all cursor-pointer ' +
    'focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-white focus-visible:outline-offset-2 shrink-0';

  const stateClass = isHighContrast
    ? 'bg-current/20 shadow-[inset_0_0_0_2px_currentColor]'
    : 'border border-current/35 bg-current/14 hover:bg-current/22 hover:border-current/50';

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onToggle}
      className={`${base} ${stateClass}`}
      aria-label="Alto contraste"
      aria-pressed={isHighContrast}
      title="Alternar modo de alto contraste"
    >
      <ContrastIcon />
    </button>
  );
}

export const PrivacyButton = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, { onClick: () => void }>(
  function PrivacyButton({ onClick }, ref) {
    const cls =
      'inline-flex items-center transition hover:opacity-80 focus-visible:outline-2 ' +
      'focus-visible:outline-white focus-visible:outline-offset-2 shrink-0 cursor-pointer';

    return (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        type="button"
        className={cls}
        aria-label="Gerenciamento de Cookies e Privacidade"
        title="Privacidade e Cookies"
        onClick={onClick}
      >
        <Cookie className="h-5 w-5 text-white" strokeWidth={2.2} aria-hidden="true" />
      </button>
    );
  }
);

export function TopBarAccessibility({
  isHighContrast,
  onToggleHighContrast,
  fontSizeScale,
  onIncreaseFontSize,
  onDecreaseFontSize,
  onResetFontSize,
  privacyButtonRef,
  onPrivacyClick,
}: TopBarAccessibilityProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-4">
      <div
        className="crasp-accessibility-toolbar flex shrink-0 flex-nowrap items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2"
        role="toolbar"
        aria-label="Ferramentas de acessibilidade"
      >
        <span className="crasp-a11y-label hidden text-xs font-medium sm:inline sm:text-sm">
          Acessibilidade
        </span>
        <div className="crasp-a11y-actions flex shrink-0 flex-nowrap items-center gap-1 sm:gap-1.5">
          <ContrastButton isHighContrast={isHighContrast} onToggle={onToggleHighContrast} />
          <TopBarFontSizeButtons
            scale={fontSizeScale}
            onIncreaseFontSize={onIncreaseFontSize}
            onDecreaseFontSize={onDecreaseFontSize}
            onResetFontSize={onResetFontSize}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
      <PrivacyButton ref={privacyButtonRef} onClick={onPrivacyClick} />
    </div>
  );
}

ARQUIVO: src/components/layout/TopBarFontSizeButtons.tsx
export function FontActionButton({
  label,
  onClick,
  ariaLabel,
  title,
}: {
  label: string;
  onClick: () => void;
  ariaLabel: string;
  title: string;
}) {
  const cls =
    'crasp-a11y-circle-btn w-7 h-7 sm:w-[1.875rem] sm:h-[1.875rem] rounded-full inline-flex ' +
    'items-center justify-center font-bold text-[0.625rem] sm:text-[0.6875rem] leading-none ' +
    'tracking-tight border border-current/35 bg-current/14 text-inherit ' +
    'hover:bg-current/22 hover:border-current/50 transition-all cursor-pointer ' +
    'focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-white focus-visible:outline-offset-2 shrink-0';

  return (
    <button type="button" onClick={onClick} className={cls} aria-label={ariaLabel} title={title}>
      {label}
    </button>
  );
}

export function FontSizeResetButton({
  active,
  onClick,
}: {
  active: boolean;
  onClick: () => void;
}) {
  const base =
    'crasp-a11y-circle-btn crasp-a11y-btn-reset w-7 h-7 sm:w-[1.875rem] sm:h-[1.875rem] rounded-full ' +
    'inline-flex items-center justify-center font-semibold text-[0.625rem] sm:text-[0.6875rem] ' +
    'leading-none tracking-tight text-inherit transition-all cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 ' +
    'focus-visible:outline-white focus-visible:outline-offset-2 shrink-0';

  const stateClass = active
    ? 'crasp-a11y-reset--active bg-current/20 shadow-[inset_0_0_0_2px_currentColor]'
    : 'border border-current/35 bg-current/14 hover:bg-current/22 hover:border-current/50';

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className={`${base} ${stateClass}`}
      aria-label="Tamanho de fonte normal"
      title="Voltar ao tamanho de fonte normal"
    >
      A
    </button>
  );
}

export function TopBarFontSizeButtons({
  scale,
  onIncreaseFontSize,
  onDecreaseFontSize,
  onResetFontSize,
}: {
  scale: number;
  onIncreaseFontSize: () => void;
  onDecreaseFontSize: () => void;
  onResetFontSize?: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <>
      <FontActionButton
        label="A+"
        onClick={onIncreaseFontSize}
        ariaLabel="Aumentar tamanho da fonte"
        title="Aumentar fonte"
      />
      <FontActionButton
        label="A-"
        onClick={onDecreaseFontSize}
        ariaLabel="Diminuir tamanho da fonte"
        title="Diminuir fonte"
      />
      {onResetFontSize && (
        <FontSizeResetButton
          active={scale === 1}
          onClick={onResetFontSize}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-blue-900">{metodologiaData.intro.title}</h2>
          </div>
          <p className="mb-6 text-lg text-gray-600 leading-relaxed">
            {metodologiaData.intro.description}
          </p>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
            {metodologiaData.pillars.map((pillar, idx) => (
              <PillarCard key={idx} pillar={pillar} />
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

    </section>
  );
}

============================================================
ARQUIVO: src/components/presentation/PresentationMode.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';

const PRESENTATION_PAGES = [
  { path: '/', label: 'Início' },
  { path: '/chancela', label: 'Chancela Institucional' },
  { path: '/metodologia', label: 'Metodologia Dinâmica' },
  { path: '/trilha', label: 'Objetivos de Aprendizagem' },
];

export async function toggleFullscreenAction() {
  try {
    if (!document.fullscreenElement) {
      await document.documentElement.requestFullscreen();
    } else {
      await document.exitFullscreen();
    }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    console.warn('[PRESENTATION_FULLSCREEN_WARN]', err);
  }
}

export function isInputFocused(e: KeyboardEvent): boolean {
  const t = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
  if (!t) return false;
  const tag = t.tagName?.toUpperCase();
  return Boolean(
    tag === 'INPUT' ||
    tag === 'TEXTAREA' ||
    tag === 'SELECT' ||
    tag === 'BUTTON' ||
    t.isContentEditable ||
    t.closest('form')
  );
}

export function isFullscreenKey(e: KeyboardEvent): boolean {
  if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) return false;
  const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
  return k === 'p' || k === 'f';
}

export function isNextKey(key: string): boolean {
  return key === 'ArrowRight' || key === 'PageDown';
}

export function isPrevKey(key: string): boolean {
  return key === 'ArrowLeft' || key === 'PageUp';
}

export function usePresentationNavigation() {
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const currentIndex = PRESENTATION_PAGES.findIndex((page) => page.path === location.pathname);

  const goToNext = useCallback(() => {
    const nextIdx = currentIndex < PRESENTATION_PAGES.length - 1 ? currentIndex + 1 : 0;
    navigate(PRESENTATION_PAGES[nextIdx].path);
  }, [currentIndex, navigate]);

  const goToPrev = useCallback(() => {
    const prevIdx = currentIndex > 0 ? currentIndex - 1 : PRESENTATION_PAGES.length - 1;
    navigate(PRESENTATION_PAGES[prevIdx].path);
  }, [currentIndex, navigate]);

  return { goToNext, goToPrev };
}

export const PresentationMode: React.FC = () => {
  const location = useLocation();
  const { goToNext, goToPrev } = usePresentationNavigation();

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (location.pathname === '/registro-interesse') return;
      if (isInputFocused(e)) return;
      if (isFullscreenKey(e)) {
        e.preventDefault();
        toggleFullscreenAction();
      } else if (isNextKey(e.key)) {
        e.preventDefault();
        goToNext();
      } else if (isPrevKey(e.key)) {
        e.preventDefault();
        goToPrev();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [location.pathname, goToNext, goToPrev]);

  return null;
};

export default PresentationMode;

ARQUIVO: src/components/presentation/useSignatoriesCarousel.ts
import { useRef, useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';

const GAP = 24;
const SCROLL_DURATION = 800;

export function calcDimensions(container: HTMLElement, itemCount: number) {
  const firstCard = container.firstElementChild as HTMLElement | null;
  const itemWidth = firstCard?.offsetWidth || 0;
  return { itemWidth, singleSetWidth: (itemWidth + GAP) * itemCount };
}

export function animateScroll(el: HTMLElement, target: number, duration: number, onEnd?: () => void) {
  const start = el.scrollLeft;
  const change = target - start;
  const t0 = performance.now();

  const frame = (t: number) => {
    if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && el.contains(document.activeElement)) {
      onEnd?.();
      return;
    }
    const p = Math.min((t - t0) / duration, 1);
    const ease = p < 0.5 ? 4 * p * p * p : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * p + 2, 3) / 2;
    el.scrollLeft = start + change * ease;
    if (p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    else onEnd?.();
  };
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}

export function balanceScrollLoop(container: HTMLElement, singleSetWidth: number) {
  if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && container.contains(document.activeElement)) return;
  const curr = container.scrollLeft;
  if (curr >= singleSetWidth * 2 - 10) {
    container.scrollLeft = curr - singleSetWidth;
  } else if (curr <= 10) {
    container.scrollLeft = curr + singleSetWidth;
  }
}

export function ensureFocusedCardVisible(container: HTMLElement, target: HTMLElement | null) {
  const card = target
    ? (Array.from(container.children).find((c) => c.contains(target)) as HTMLElement)
    : null;
  if (!card) return;
  const cardLeft = card.offsetLeft;
  const cardRight = cardLeft + card.offsetWidth;
  if (cardLeft < container.scrollLeft) {
    container.scrollLeft = cardLeft;
  } else if (cardRight > container.scrollLeft + container.clientWidth) {
    container.scrollLeft = cardRight - container.clientWidth;
  }
}

export function useCarouselAutoLoop(
  ref: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>,
  itemCount: number,
  onTick: () => void
) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (ref.current) {
      const { singleSetWidth } = calcDimensions(ref.current, itemCount);
      ref.current.scrollLeft = singleSetWidth;
    }
    const prefersReduced =
      typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
      window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    if (prefersReduced) return;

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && ref.current?.contains(document.activeElement)) return;
      onTick();
    }, 5000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [ref, itemCount, onTick]);
}

export function useCarouselNavigation(
  ref: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>,
  itemCount: number,
  smoothScrollTo: (t: number) => void
) {
  const scrollToDot = useCallback((idx: number) => {
    if (!ref.current) return;
    const { itemWidth, singleSetWidth } = calcDimensions(ref.current, itemCount);
    smoothScrollTo(Math.floor(ref.current.scrollLeft / singleSetWidth) * singleSetWidth + idx * (itemWidth + GAP));
  }, [ref, itemCount, smoothScrollTo]);

  const scroll = useCallback((dir: 'left' | 'right') => {
    if (!ref.current) return;
    const { itemWidth } = calcDimensions(ref.current, itemCount);
    smoothScrollTo(ref.current.scrollLeft + (itemWidth + GAP) * (dir === 'right' ? 1 : -1));
  }, [ref, itemCount, smoothScrollTo]);

  return { scrollToDot, scroll };
}

export function useSmoothScroll(ref: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>) {
  const isScrollingRef = useRef(false);
  const smoothScrollTo = useCallback((target: number) => {
    if (!ref.current) return;
    isScrollingRef.current = true;
    animateScroll(ref.current, target, SCROLL_DURATION, () => {
      isScrollingRef.current = false;
    });
  }, [ref]);
  return { smoothScrollTo, isScrollingRef };
}

export function useScrollWatcher(
  ref: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>,
  itemCount: number,
  isScrollingRef: React.RefObject<boolean>
) {
  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);
  const timeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout>(undefined);

  const handleScroll = useCallback(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const { itemWidth, singleSetWidth } = calcDimensions(el, itemCount);
    if (!itemWidth) return;
    setActiveIndex(Math.round(el.scrollLeft / (itemWidth + GAP)) % itemCount);
    if (timeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(timeoutRef.current);
    timeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      if (!ref.current || isScrollingRef.current) return;
      balanceScrollLoop(ref.current, singleSetWidth);
    }, 150);
  }, [ref, itemCount, isScrollingRef]);

  const handleFocus = useCallback((e: React.FocusEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (!ref.current) return;
    ensureFocusedCardVisible(ref.current, e.target as HTMLElement);
  }, [ref]);

  return { activeIndex, handleScroll, handleFocus };
}

export function useSignatoriesCarousel(itemCount: number) {
  const scrollContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const { smoothScrollTo, isScrollingRef } = useSmoothScroll(scrollContainerRef);
  const { activeIndex, handleScroll, handleFocus } = useScrollWatcher(
    scrollContainerRef,
    itemCount,
    isScrollingRef
  );
  const { scrollToDot, scroll } = useCarouselNavigation(scrollContainerRef, itemCount, smoothScrollTo);

  useCarouselAutoLoop(scrollContainerRef, itemCount, useCallback(() => scroll('right'), [scroll]));

  return { scrollContainerRef, activeIndex, handleScroll, handleFocus, scrollToDot, scroll };
}

ARQUIVO: src/components/ui/Badge.tsx
import React from 'react';

export type BadgeVariant = 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'blue-light' | 'ghost' | 'outline';

interface BadgeProps extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLSpanElement> {
  variant?: BadgeVariant;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

export const Badge = ({ variant = 'primary', className = '', children, ...props }: BadgeProps) => {
  const baseStyles = "inline-flex items-center font-semibold";
  
  const variants = {
    primary: "rounded-full bg-blue-100 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-blue-800",
    secondary:
      'rounded-full bg-gray-100 px-3 py-1 text-sm text-gray-700 ' +
      'hover:bg-blue-100 hover:text-blue-800 transition-colors',
    'blue-light': "rounded-full bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-blue-800",
    ghost: "uppercase tracking-wide text-sm text-blue-300",
    outline: "uppercase tracking-wider text-xs text-slate-500",
  };

  return (
    <span className={`${baseStyles} ${variants[variant]} ${className}`.trim()} {...props}>
      {children}
    </span>
  );
};

============================================================
ARQUIVO: src/components/ui/Button.tsx
import React from 'react';

export type ButtonVariant = 'primary' | 'outline-white' | 'ghost' | 'icon-outline' | 'white' | 'yellow';
export type ButtonSize = 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'icon' | 'none';

interface ButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  size?: ButtonSize;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

export const buttonStyles = (variant: ButtonVariant = 'primary', size: ButtonSize = 'md', className: string = '') => {
  const baseStyles =
    'inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 font-semibold transition-all focus:outline-none ' +
    'focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-800 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 disabled:opacity-50 ' +
    'disabled:pointer-events-none';
  
  const variants = {
    primary: "bg-blue-800 text-white shadow-sm hover:bg-blue-900 rounded-full",
    yellow: "bg-yellow-500 text-black shadow-sm hover:bg-yellow-400 rounded-full",
    'outline-white': "border border-white text-white hover:bg-white/10 rounded-full",
    ghost: "text-blue-800 hover:text-blue-900",
    'icon-outline':
      'border border-gray-200 bg-white text-gray-600 shadow-sm ' +
      'hover:border-blue-800 hover:text-blue-800 rounded-full',
    white: "bg-white text-blue-900 shadow-sm hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-full",
  };

  const sizes = {
    sm: "px-4 py-2 text-sm",
    md: "px-6 py-3 text-sm",
    lg: "px-8 py-4 text-base",
    icon: "h-10 w-10 md:h-11 md:w-11",
    none: "",
  };

  const appliedSize = variant === 'ghost' ? '' : sizes[size];

  return `${baseStyles} ${variants[variant]} ${appliedSize} ${className}`.trim();
};

export const Button = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className = '', variant = 'primary', size = 'md', children, ...props }, ref) => {
    return (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        className={buttonStyles(variant as ButtonVariant, size as ButtonSize, className)}
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
      </button>
    );
  }
);

Button.displayName = 'Button';

============================================================
ARQUIVO: src/components/ui/Card.tsx
import React from 'react';

interface CardProps extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  hoverable?: boolean;
  className?: string;
}

export const Card = React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, CardProps>(
  ({ className = '', hoverable = false, children, ...props }, ref) => {
    const hasBgClass = className.includes('bg-');
    const baseStyles = `rounded-2xl ${hasBgClass ? '' : 'bg-white'} shadow-sm ring-1 ring-gray-100`;
    const hoverStyles = hoverable ? "transition-all hover:shadow-md hover:ring-blue-200" : "";
    
    return (
      <div ref={ref} className={`${baseStyles} ${hoverStyles} ${className}`.trim()} {...props}>
        {children}
      </div>
    );
  }
);

Card.displayName = 'Card';

============================================================
ARQUIVO: src/components/ui/SectionHeader.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from './Button';

interface SectionHeaderProps {
  title: string;
  linkText?: string;
  linkUrl?: string;
  linkClassName?: string;
  className?: string;
  titleClassName?: string;
  onLinkClick?: () => void;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
}

export function SectionHeaderAnimatedLink({
  linkUrl,
  linkText,
  linkClassName,
}: {
  linkUrl: string;
  linkText: string;
  linkClassName: string;
}) {
  return (
    <a
      href={linkUrl}
      className={
        'group inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-sm font-semibold ' +
        `text-blue-800 hover:text-blue-900 ${linkClassName}`.trim()
      }
    >
      <span className="leading-none">{linkText}</span>
      <span className="flex items-center relative top-[1px] animate-pulse-chevron" aria-hidden="true">
        <ChevronRight size={16} />
      </span>
    </a>
  );
}

export function SectionHeaderAction({
  linkText,
  linkUrl,
  linkClassName = '',
  onLinkClick,
}: {
  linkText?: string;
  linkUrl?: string;
  linkClassName?: string;
  onLinkClick?: () => void;
}) {
  if (!linkText) return null;
  if (!linkUrl) {
    return (
      <Button variant="ghost" className={linkClassName} onClick={onLinkClick}>
        {linkText}
      </Button>
    );
  }
  return (
    <SectionHeaderAnimatedLink
      linkUrl={linkUrl}
      linkText={linkText}
      linkClassName={linkClassName}
    />
  );
}

export const SectionHeader = ({
  title,
  linkText,
  linkUrl,
  linkClassName = '',
  className = 'mb-6',
  titleClassName = 'text-gray-900',
  onLinkClick,
  children,
}: SectionHeaderProps) => {
  return (
    <div className={`flex items-center justify-between ${className}`.trim()}>
      <h2 className={`text-xl font-bold ${titleClassName}`}>{title}</h2>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
        {children}
        <SectionHeaderAction
          linkText={linkText}
          linkUrl={linkUrl}
          linkClassName={linkClassName}
          onLinkClick={onLinkClick}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

============================================================
ARQUIVO: src/hooks/useAccessibilitySync.ts
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useAccessibilityStore } from '../store/useAccessibilityStore';

export function useAccessibilitySync() {
  const acc = useAccessibilityStore();

  useEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    root.dataset.fontScale = String(acc.fontSizeScale);
    root.classList.toggle('alto-contraste', acc.isHighContrast);
  }, [acc.fontSizeScale, acc.isHighContrast]);

  return acc;
}

export function InterestRegistrationPage() {
  const [submittedData, setSubmittedData] = useState<{
    nome: string;
    instituicao: string;
  } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.title = 'Manifestação de Interesse | CRA-SP';
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, left: 0, behavior: 'instant' });
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col lg:flex-row bg-white font-sans">
      <LoginBrandingColumn />
      <main
        className={
          'w-full lg:w-1/2 flex flex-col justify-center px-6 sm:px-10 lg:px-10 xl:px-14 ' +
          'py-10 min-h-screen overflow-y-auto'
        }
      >
        <div className="w-full max-w-xl mx-auto">
          <BackToHomeLink />
          {submittedData ? (
            <InterestSuccessView nome={submittedData.nome} instituicao={submittedData.instituicao} />
          ) : (
            <InterestForm onSuccess={(data) => setSubmittedData(data)} />
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

          <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
        </Route>
      </Routes>
    </Suspense>
  );
};

ARQUIVO: src/server/leadBucketService.ts
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import type { Firestore } from 'firebase-admin/firestore';

export const MAX_BUCKET_BYTES = 943718;

export interface BucketData {
  numeroParte: number;
  criadoEm: string;
  atualizadoEm: string;
  totalRegistros: number;
  tamanhoAproximadoBytes: number;
  manifestacoes: Record<string, unknown>[];
}

export function getEmailHash(email: string): string {
  const pepper = process.env.HMAC_PEPPER_KEY;
  if (!pepper) {
    throw new Error('Variável obrigatória de segurança HMAC_PEPPER_KEY ausente.');
  }
  return crypto.createHmac('sha256', pepper).update(email.toLowerCase().trim()).digest('hex');
}

export function sanitizeErrorMessage(rawMsg: string): string {
  return rawMsg
    .slice(0, 2000)
    .replace(/[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+/g, '[REDACTED_EMAIL]')
    .replace(/\(?\d{2}\)?\s?\d{4,5}-?\d{4}/g, '[REDACTED_PHONE]')
    .replace(/\b\d{3}\.?\d{3}\.?\d{3}-?\d{2}\b/g, '[REDACTED_CPF]')
    .replace(/leads_adesao\/[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+/g, 'leads_adesao/[REDACTED_DOC]');
}

export function buildBucketPayload(
  partNum: number,
  bucket: BucketData | null,
  lead: Record<string, unknown>
): BucketData {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const manifestacoes = bucket ? [...(bucket.manifestacoes || []), lead] : [lead];
  return {
    numeroParte: partNum,
    criadoEm: bucket?.criadoEm || now,
    atualizadoEm: now,
    totalRegistros: manifestacoes.length,
    tamanhoAproximadoBytes: Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(manifestacoes), 'utf8'),
    manifestacoes,
  };
}

function checkBucketFit(
  bucket: BucketData,
  emailHash: string,
  leadBytes: number
): { isDup: boolean; canFit: boolean } {
  const isDup = (bucket.manifestacoes || []).some(
    (m) => ((m?.emailHash as string) || '') === emailHash
  );
  if (isDup) return { isDup: true, canFit: false };
  const curSize = Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(bucket), 'utf8');
  return { isDup: false, canFit: curSize + leadBytes <= MAX_BUCKET_BYTES };
}

function writeDualRecords(
  tx: FirebaseFirestore.Transaction,
  refs: {
    indexRef: FirebaseFirestore.DocumentReference;
    docRef: FirebaseFirestore.DocumentReference;
    vaultRef: FirebaseFirestore.DocumentReference;
  },
  data: {
    indexData: Record<string, unknown>;
    vaultLead: Record<string, unknown> | null;
    bucketPayload: BucketData;
  }
): void {
  tx.set(refs.indexRef, data.indexData);
  if (data.vaultLead) tx.set(refs.vaultRef, data.vaultLead);
  tx.set(refs.docRef, data.bucketPayload);
}

interface SavePartParams {
  partNum: number;
  opLead: Record<string, unknown>;
  vaultLead: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  email: string;
  leadBytes: number;
}

interface BucketDocRefs {
  indexRef: FirebaseFirestore.DocumentReference;
  docRef: FirebaseFirestore.DocumentReference;
  vaultRef: FirebaseFirestore.DocumentReference;
}

function handleExistingBucket(
  tx: FirebaseFirestore.Transaction,
  refs: BucketDocRefs,
  p: SavePartParams,
  snap: FirebaseFirestore.DocumentSnapshot,
  meta: { docId: string; hash: string; leadId: string }
): { done: boolean; success: boolean; duplicate: boolean; docId: string } {
  const { isDup, canFit } = checkBucketFit(snap.data() as BucketData, meta.hash, p.leadBytes);
  if (isDup) {
    const existing = (snap.data()?.manifestacoes || []).find(
      (m: Record<string, unknown>) => ((m?.emailHash as string) || '') === meta.hash
    );
    const existingLeadId = (existing?.leadId as string) || meta.leadId;
    tx.set(refs.indexRef, { criadoEm: new Date().toISOString(), parte: meta.docId, leadId: existingLeadId });
    return { done: true, success: false, duplicate: true, docId: existingLeadId };
  }
  if (canFit) {
    const bucketPayload = buildBucketPayload(p.partNum, snap.data() as BucketData, p.opLead);
    const indexData = { criadoEm: new Date().toISOString(), parte: meta.docId, leadId: meta.leadId };
    writeDualRecords(tx, refs, { indexData, vaultLead: p.vaultLead, bucketPayload });
    return { done: true, success: true, duplicate: false, docId: meta.leadId };
  }
  return { done: false, success: false, duplicate: false, docId: '' };
}

async function trySaveLeadInPart(
  db: Firestore,
  p: SavePartParams
): Promise<{ done: boolean; success: boolean; duplicate: boolean; docId: string }> {
  const docId = `parte${p.partNum}`;
  const docRef = db.collection('leads_adesao').doc(docId);
  const hash = getEmailHash(p.email);
  const indexRef = db.collection('leads_unicidade').doc(hash);
  const leadId = (p.opLead.leadId as string) || (p.opLead.id as string) || `lead_${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
  const refs = { indexRef, docRef, vaultRef: db.collection('vault_leads').doc(leadId) };

  return db.runTransaction(async (tx) => {
    if ((await tx.get(indexRef)).exists) {
      return { done: true, success: false, duplicate: true, docId: '' };
    }
    const snap = await tx.get(docRef);
    if (!snap.exists) {
      const bucketPayload = buildBucketPayload(p.partNum, null, p.opLead);
      const indexData = { criadoEm: new Date().toISOString(), parte: docId, leadId };
      writeDualRecords(tx, refs, { indexData, vaultLead: p.vaultLead, bucketPayload });
      return { done: true, success: true, duplicate: false, docId: leadId };
    }
    return handleExistingBucket(tx, refs, p, snap, { docId, hash, leadId });
  });
}

export async function writeLeadToBucket(
  db: Firestore | null,
  operationalLead: Record<string, unknown>,
  email: string,
  vaultLead: Record<string, unknown> | null = null
): Promise<{ success: boolean; duplicate: boolean; docId: string }> {
  if (!db) return { success: false, duplicate: false, docId: '' };
  const targetEmail = email.toLowerCase();
  try {
    const leadBytes = Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(operationalLead), 'utf8');
    let partNum = 1;

    while (true) {
      const res = await trySaveLeadInPart(db, {
        partNum, opLead: operationalLead, vaultLead, email: targetEmail, leadBytes,
      });
      if (res.done) {
        return { success: res.success, duplicate: res.duplicate, docId: res.docId };
      }
      partNum += 1;
    }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const raw = String((err as Error)?.message || '');
    console.warn('[FIREBASE_WARN] Falha ao gravar no bucket do Firestore:', sanitizeErrorMessage(raw));
    return { success: false, duplicate: false, docId: '' };
  }
}

export const DEV_CSP_HEADER = [
  "default-src 'none'",
  "script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' 'unsafe-eval' https://vlibras.gov.br " +
    'https://*.vlibras.gov.br https://cdn.jsdelivr.net',
  "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' https://fonts.googleapis.com https://vlibras.gov.br " +
    'https://*.vlibras.gov.br https://cdn.jsdelivr.net',
  "style-src-attr 'unsafe-inline'",
  "font-src 'self' https://fonts.gstatic.com https://vlibras.gov.br " +
    'https://*.vlibras.gov.br https://cdn.jsdelivr.net',
  "img-src 'self' blob: data: https://vlibras.gov.br https://*.vlibras.gov.br " +
    'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net https://images.unsplash.com ' +
    'https://firebasestorage.googleapis.com https://crasp-frontend.vercel.app ' +
    'https://hackathonenangrad.espm.edu.br https://www.ethos.org.br https://ju.se',
  "connect-src 'self' ws: wss: http: https: https://*.googleapis.com https://*.firebaseio.com " +
    'https://vlibras.gov.br https://*.vlibras.gov.br https://cdn.jsdelivr.net',
  "frame-src 'self' https://vlibras.gov.br https://*.vlibras.gov.br " +
    'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net',
  "worker-src 'self' blob: https://vlibras.gov.br https://*.vlibras.gov.br " +
    'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net',
  "object-src 'none'",
  "base-uri 'self'",
  "form-action 'self'",
  "upgrade-insecure-requests",
  "frame-ancestors 'self' https://aistudio.google.com https://*.google.com https://*.run.app https://ai.studio",
].join('; ');

export function getActiveCsp(): string {
  return process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? CSP_HEADER : DEV_CSP_HEADER;
}

export function formatSecureCookie(c: unknown): string {
  let str = String(c);
  if (!/;\s*Secure/i.test(str)) str += '; Secure';
  if (!/;\s*SameSite/i.test(str)) str += '; SameSite=Lax';
  if (!/;\s*HttpOnly/i.test(str)) str += '; HttpOnly';
  return str;
}

export function applySecurityHeaders(_req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
  res.setHeader('Strict-Transport-Security', 'max-age=63072000; includeSubDomains; preload');
  res.setHeader('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff');
  res.setHeader('Referrer-Policy', 'no-referrer');
  res.setHeader('Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy', 'same-origin-allow-popups');
  res.setHeader('Cross-Origin-Embedder-Policy', 'credentialless');

  res.setHeader('Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy', 'same-origin');
  res.setHeader(
    'Permissions-Policy',
    'camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=(), payment=(), usb=(), browsing-topics=()'
  );

  res.setHeader('Content-Security-Policy', getActiveCsp());

  const origSet = res.setHeader.bind(res);
  res.setHeader = (name: string, value: number | string | readonly string[]) => {
    if (typeof name === 'string' && name.toLowerCase() === 'set-cookie') {
      const list = Array.isArray(value) ? value : [value];
      const secured = list.map(formatSecureCookie);
      return origSet(name, Array.isArray(value) ? secured : secured[0]);
    }
    return origSet(name, value);
  };

  next();
}

============================================================
ARQUIVO: src/server/vaultService.ts
import crypto from 'node:crypto';

function getVaultKey(): Buffer {
  const secret = process.env.VAULT_ENCRYPTION_KEY;
  if (!secret) {
    throw new Error('Variável obrigatória de segurança VAULT_ENCRYPTION_KEY ausente.');
  }
  return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(secret).digest();
}

export function encryptVaultField(text: string): string {
  if (!text) return '';
  const key = getVaultKey();
  const iv = crypto.randomBytes(12);
  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv('aes-256-gcm', key, iv);
  const enc = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(text, 'utf8'), cipher.final()]);
  const tag = cipher.getAuthTag();
  return `v1:${iv.toString('base64')}:${tag.toString('base64')}:${enc.toString('base64')}`;
}

export function decryptVaultField(cipherText: string): string {
  if (!cipherText || !cipherText.startsWith('v1:')) return cipherText;
  try {
    const parts = cipherText.split(':');
    if (parts.length !== 4) return cipherText;
    const [, ivB64, tagB64, encB64] = parts;
    const key = getVaultKey();
    const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv('aes-256-gcm', key, Buffer.from(ivB64, 'base64'));
    decipher.setAuthTag(Buffer.from(tagB64, 'base64'));
    return decipher.update(Buffer.from(encB64, 'base64')) + decipher.final('utf8');
  } catch {
    return '[ENCRYPTED_VAULT_PAYLOAD]';
  }
}

export function buildVaultRecord(
  data: { nome: string; email: string; telefone: string; observacoes?: string },
  docId: string
) {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  return {
    leadId: docId,
    nome: encryptVaultField(data.nome),
    email: encryptVaultField(data.email),
    telefone: encryptVaultField(data.telefone),
    observacoes: encryptVaultField(data.observacoes || ''),
    criadoEm: now,
    vaultEncrypted: true,
  };
}

export function buildOperationalRecord(
  data: {
    email: string;
    instituicao: string;
    campus: string;
    cargo: string;
    curso: string;
    previsaoSemestre: string;
    observacoes?: string;
  },
  docId: string,
  emailHash: string
) {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  return {
    leadId: docId,
    emailHash,
    instituicao: data.instituicao,
    campus: data.campus,
    cargo: data.cargo,
    curso: data.curso,
    previsaoSemestre: data.previsaoSemestre,
    temObservacoes: Boolean(data.observacoes?.trim()),
    origem: 'portal_cra_sp',
    status: 'pendente_analise',
    criadoEm: now,
    vaultRef: `vault_leads/${docId}`,
  };
}

ARQUIVO: src/store/useAccessibilityStore.ts
import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';

export interface AccessibilityState {
  fontSizeScale: number;
  isHighContrast: boolean;
}

const STORAGE_KEY = 'crasp_a11y_state';

const DEFAULT_STATE: AccessibilityState = {
  fontSizeScale: 1.0,
  isHighContrast: false,
};

function parseFontSize(val: unknown): number {
  const n = Number(val);
  return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1.0 && n <= 1.2 ? n : 1.0;
}

function parseStoredState(raw: string | null): AccessibilityState {
  if (!raw) return DEFAULT_STATE;
  try {
    const p = JSON.parse(raw);
    return {
      fontSizeScale: parseFontSize(p.fontSizeScale),
      isHighContrast: Boolean(p.isHighContrast),
    };
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    console.warn('[ACCESSIBILITY_PARSE_WARN]', err);
    return DEFAULT_STATE;
  }
}

function loadInitialState(): AccessibilityState {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return DEFAULT_STATE;
  try {
    return parseStoredState(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY));
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    console.warn('[ACCESSIBILITY_LOAD_WARN]', err);
    return DEFAULT_STATE;
  }
}

let currentState: AccessibilityState = loadInitialState();
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

function persistState(state: AccessibilityState): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    console.warn('[ACCESSIBILITY_PERSIST_WARN]', err);
  }
}

function emitChange(nextState: AccessibilityState): void {
  currentState = nextState;
  listeners.forEach((listener) => listener());
  persistState(nextState);
}

export const accessibilityStore = {
  getState(): AccessibilityState {
    return currentState;
  },
  subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void {
    listeners.add(listener);
    return () => listeners.delete(listener);
  },
  toggleHighContrast(): void {
    emitChange({
      ...currentState,
      isHighContrast: !currentState.isHighContrast,
    });
  },
  increaseFontSize(): void {
    const next = Math.min(1.2, +(currentState.fontSizeScale + 0.1).toFixed(1));
    emitChange({ ...currentState, fontSizeScale: next });
  },
  decreaseFontSize(): void {
    const next = Math.max(1.0, +(currentState.fontSizeScale - 0.1).toFixed(1));
    emitChange({ ...currentState, fontSizeScale: next });
  },
  resetFontSize(): void {
    emitChange({ ...currentState, fontSizeScale: 1.0 });
  },
};

export function useAccessibilityStore() {
  const state = useSyncExternalStore(
    accessibilityStore.subscribe,
    accessibilityStore.getState,
    accessibilityStore.getState
  );
  return {
    ...state,
    toggleHighContrast: accessibilityStore.toggleHighContrast,
    increaseFontSize: accessibilityStore.increaseFontSize,
    decreaseFontSize: accessibilityStore.decreaseFontSize,
    resetFontSize: accessibilityStore.resetFontSize,
  };
}

ARQUIVO: src/types/leads.ts
import { z } from 'zod';

export const LeadRegistrationInputSchema = z
  .object({
    nome: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .min(2, 'O nome deve conter pelo menos 2 caracteres.')
      .max(150, 'O nome deve conter no máximo 150 caracteres.'),
    email: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase()
      .email('Endereço de e-mail institucional inválido.')
      .max(150, 'O e-mail deve conter no máximo 150 caracteres.'),
    telefone: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .regex(/^\D*(?:\d\D*){10,11}$/, 'Telefone deve conter DDD e 8 ou 9 dígitos.')
      .max(30, 'Telefone deve conter no máximo 30 caracteres.'),
    instituicao: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .min(2, 'A instituição deve conter pelo menos 2 caracteres.')
      .max(200, 'A instituição deve conter no máximo 200 caracteres.'),
    campus: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .min(1, 'O campus é obrigatório.')
      .max(100, 'O campus deve conter no máximo 100 caracteres.'),
    cargo: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .max(100, 'O cargo deve conter no máximo 100 caracteres.')
      .optional()
      .default(''),
    curso: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .max(100, 'O curso deve conter no máximo 100 caracteres.')
      .optional()
      .default(''),
    previsaoSemestre: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .min(1, 'A previsão de implementação é obrigatória.')
      .max(100, 'A previsão deve conter no máximo 100 caracteres.'),
    observacoes: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .max(1000, 'As observações devem conter no máximo 1000 caracteres.')
      .optional()
      .default(''),
  })
  .strict();

export type LeadRegistrationInput = z.infer<typeof LeadRegistrationInputSchema>;

export interface LeadRegistrationRecord extends LeadRegistrationInput {
  origem: string;
  criadoEm: unknown;
}

ARQUIVO: tests/check-metrics.test.ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import ts from 'typescript';
import {
  isDeclarativeAST,
  getCyclomaticComplexity,
  getCognitiveComplexity,
  getLogicLines,
} from '../scripts/metrics-ast';
import { runMetricsAudit } from '../scripts/metrics-runner';

describe('1. Validação Declarativa AST', () => {
  it('deve identificar arquivos puramente declarativos sem construções imperativas', () => {
    const declCode = 'export const CONFIG = { a: 1 }; export type MyType = { id: string };';
    const sfDecl = ts.createSourceFile('decl.ts', declCode, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true);
    expect(isDeclarativeAST(sfDecl)).toBe(true);

    const imperCode = 'export function calc(x: number) { if (x > 0) return x * 2; return 0; }';
    const sfImper = ts.createSourceFile('imper.ts', imperCode, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true);
    expect(isDeclarativeAST(sfImper)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('2. Validação CC com Isolamento', () => {
  it('deve calcular CC isolando estritamente funções internas', () => {
    const code =
      'export function outer() {\n' +
      '  const inner = (x: number) => { if (x > 10) return 1; return 2; };\n' +
      '  return inner(1);\n' +
      '}';
    const sf = ts.createSourceFile('cc.ts', code, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true);
    const outerFn = sf.statements[0] as ts.FunctionDeclaration;
    expect(getCyclomaticComplexity(outerFn)).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe('3. Validação Complexidade Cognitiva', () => {
  it('deve calcular Complexidade Cognitiva de acordo com SonarSource White Paper', () => {
    const code =
      'function sample(a: boolean, b: boolean, c: boolean, n: number) {\n' +
      '  if (a && b || c) { for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {} }\n' +
      '}';
    const sf = ts.createSourceFile('cog.ts', code, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true);
    const fn = sf.statements[0] as ts.FunctionDeclaration;
    expect(getCognitiveComplexity(fn)).toBe(5);
  });
});

describe('4. Validação Linhas de Lógica', () => {
  it('deve calcular linhas de lógica desconsiderando assinaturas multiline e chaves isoladas', () => {
    const code =
      'export function fn(a: string, b: number): boolean {\n' +
      '  // comment\n' +
      '  const x = a.trim();\n' +
      '  return x.length > b;\n' +
      '}';
    const sf = ts.createSourceFile('logic.ts', code, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true);
    const fn = sf.statements[0] as ts.FunctionDeclaration;
    expect(getLogicLines(fn, sf)).toBe(2);
  });
});

describe('5. Auditoria Integral do Repositório (Incluindo Scripts)', () => {
  it('deve certificar que 100% dos arquivos e funções atendem aos limites do AGENTS.MD', () => {
    const audit = runMetricsAudit();
    expect(audit.errors).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(audit.evaluatedFunctions).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(300);
    expect(audit.maxFileLines).toBeLessThanOrEqual(250);
    expect(audit.maxLineLength).toBeLessThanOrEqual(120);
    expect(audit.maxPhysical).toBeLessThanOrEqual(40);
    expect(audit.maxLogic).toBeLessThanOrEqual(25);
    expect(audit.maxCC).toBeLessThanOrEqual(7);
    expect(audit.maxCog).toBeLessThanOrEqual(15);
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import fs from 'node:fs';

describe('4. Auditoria de Segurança das Regras do Firestore (firestore.rules)', () => {
  const rules = fs.readFileSync('firestore.rules', 'utf8');

  it('não deve conter permissões públicas abertas ou condicionais permissivas', () => {
    expect(rules).not.toMatch(/allow\s+(read|write|read,\s*write)\s*:\s*if\s+true/i);
    expect(rules).not.toMatch(/allow\s+read\s*:\s*if\s+request\.time\s*!=\s*null/i);
    expect(rules).not.toMatch(/allow\s+create/i);
    expect(rules).not.toMatch(/allow\s+update/i);
  });

  it('deve conter deny-by-default estrito para todos os documentos do cliente', () => {
    expect(rules).toContain('match /{document=**}');
    expect(rules).toContain('allow read, write: if false;');
  });
});

    expect(res.status).toBe(503);
    expect(res.body.error).toBe('SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE');

describe('4.1 Limite de Tráfego e Descoberta ARD', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    rateLimitMap.clear();
  });

  it('deve responder 429 quando limite for excedido', async () => {
    const ip = '192.168.1.100';
    rateLimitMap.set(ip, { count: 15, resetAt: Date.now() + 60000 });
    const res = await request(app).post('/api/registrar-interesse')
      .set('X-Forwarded-For', ip).send({ nome: 'Incompleto' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(429);
  });

  it('deve servir manifesto ARD válido em /.well-known/ard.json com JSON', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/.well-known/ard.json');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.headers['content-type']).toContain('application/json');
    expect(res.body.entries).toBeInstanceOf(Array);
  });
});

describe('5. Rate Limiter e Sanitização', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    rateLimitMap.clear();
  });

  it('deve conter mapa e expirar entradas', () => {
    const now = Date.now();
    rateLimitMap.set('expirado', { count: 1, resetAt: now - 1000 });
    cleanupExpiredRateLimits(now);
    expect(rateLimitMap.has('expirado')).toBe(false);
    expect(MAX_MAP_ENTRIES).toBe(5000);
  });

  it('deve preservar IP da requisição atual durante a poda de contenção', () => {
    const currentIp = '10.0.0.99';
    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) {
      rateLimitMap.set(`ip-${i}`, { count: 1, resetAt: Date.now() + 60000 });
    }
    rateLimitMap.set(currentIp, { count: 1, resetAt: Date.now() + 60000 });
    pruneOldestEntries(10, currentIp);
    expect(rateLimitMap.has(currentIp)).toBe(true);
    expect(rateLimitMap.size).toBeLessThanOrEqual(11);
  });

  it('deve sanitizar e-mail e documento de identificação em logs de erro', () => {
    const raw = 'Erro leads_adesao/doc-123 para carlos@ies.edu.br tel (11) 98765-4321';
    const sanitized = sanitizeErrorMessage(raw);
    expect(sanitized).not.toContain('carlos@ies.edu.br');
    expect(sanitized).not.toContain('doc-123');
    expect(sanitized).toContain('[REDACTED_EMAIL]');
    expect(sanitized).toContain('[REDACTED_PHONE]');
    expect(sanitized).toContain('leads_adesao/[REDACTED_DOC]');
  });
});

describe('6. Padrão Normativo CRA-SP (Bucket Pattern parteN e Limite de 90%)', () => {
  it('deve respeitar a cota de 90% de 1 MiB (943.718 bytes)', () => {
    expect(MAX_BUCKET_BYTES).toBe(943718);
    expect(MAX_BUCKET_BYTES).toBe(Math.floor(1024 * 1024 * 0.9));
  });

  it('deve montar estrutura de agregação parte1 com manifestações', () => {
    const lead = { nome: 'Prof. Exemplo', email: 'ex@ies.edu.br' };
    const bucket = buildBucketPayload(1, null, lead);
    expect(bucket.numeroParte).toBe(1);
    expect(bucket.totalRegistros).toBe(1);
    expect(bucket.manifestacoes).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(bucket.manifestacoes[0]).toEqual(lead);
    expect(bucket.tamanhoAproximadoBytes).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('deve incrementar manifestações mantendo data de criação original', () => {
    const lead1 = { nome: 'Prof. 1', email: 'p1@ies.edu.br' };
    const lead2 = { nome: 'Prof. 2', email: 'p2@ies.edu.br' };
    const b1 = buildBucketPayload(1, null, lead1);
    const b2 = buildBucketPayload(1, b1, lead2);
    expect(b2.numeroParte).toBe(1);
    expect(b2.totalRegistros).toBe(2);
    expect(b2.criadoEm).toBe(b1.criadoEm);
    expect(b2.manifestacoes).toHaveLength(2);
  });
});

describe('7. Cabeçalhos de Segurança HTTP (Mozilla Observatory)', () => {
  it('deve aplicar todos os cabeçalhos de segurança obrigatórios e omitir x-powered-by', async () => {
    const prev = process.env.NODE_ENV;
    process.env.NODE_ENV = 'production';
    const res = await request(app).post('/api/registrar-interesse').send({});
    process.env.NODE_ENV = prev;
    expect(res.headers['strict-transport-security']).toBe(
      'max-age=63072000; includeSubDomains; preload'
    );
    expect(res.headers['cross-origin-opener-policy']).toBe('same-origin-allow-popups');
    expect(res.headers['cross-origin-embedder-policy']).toBe('credentialless');
    expect(res.headers['content-security-policy']).toContain("frame-ancestors 'self'");
    expect(res.headers['x-content-type-options']).toBe('nosniff');
    expect(res.headers['referrer-policy']).toBe('no-referrer');
    expect(res.headers['content-security-policy']).toContain("object-src 'none'");
    expect(res.headers['content-security-policy']).toContain("base-uri 'self'");
    expect(res.headers['content-security-policy']).toContain("form-action 'self'");
    expect(res.headers['content-security-policy']).toContain("upgrade-insecure-requests");
    expect(res.headers['cross-origin-resource-policy']).toBe('same-origin');
    expect(res.headers['permissions-policy']).toContain('camera=()');
    expect(res.headers['x-powered-by']).toBeUndefined();
    expect(res.headers['content-security-policy']).not.toMatch(/(^|;\s*)style-src\s+[^;]*'unsafe-inline'/);
    expect(res.headers['content-security-policy']).toContain('vlibras.gov.br');
  });

  it('deve validar Subresource Integrity (SRI sha384) nos assets de dist/index.html', () => {
    if (!fs.existsSync('dist/index.html')) return;
    const html = fs.readFileSync('dist/index.html', 'utf8');
    expect(html).toContain('integrity="sha384-');
  });
});

ARQUIVO: tests/vault.test.ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  encryptVaultField, decryptVaultField, buildVaultRecord, buildOperationalRecord,
} from '../src/server/vaultService';
import { getEmailHash } from '../src/server/leadBucketService';

describe('8.1 Auditoria de Ausência de Segredos Hardcoded', () => {
  it('deve lançar erro fatal se variáveis obrigatórias estiverem ausentes', () => {
    delete process.env.HMAC_PEPPER_KEY;
    delete process.env.VAULT_ENCRYPTION_KEY;
    expect(() => getEmailHash('teste@ies.edu.br')).toThrow(/HMAC_PEPPER_KEY ausente/);
    expect(() => encryptVaultField('Nome Teste')).toThrow(/VAULT_ENCRYPTION_KEY ausente/);
  });
});

describe('8.2 Auditoria Criptográfica AES-256-GCM e HMAC-SHA256', () => {
  const pepper = 'pepper_teste_hmac_secret_key_2026';
  const vaultKey = 'chave_teste_vault_secret_32bytes_!';

  it('deve cifrar e decifrar PII sensível com tag de autenticação e IV aleatório', () => {
    process.env.VAULT_ENCRYPTION_KEY = vaultKey;
    const textoOriginal = 'Prof. Carlos Eduardo da Silva';
    const c1 = encryptVaultField(textoOriginal);
    const c2 = encryptVaultField(textoOriginal);

    expect(c1).toMatch(/^v1:[A-Za-z0-9+/=]+:[A-Za-z0-9+/=]+:[A-Za-z0-9+/=]+$/);
    expect(c1).not.toBe(c2);
    expect(c1).not.toContain(textoOriginal);
    expect(decryptVaultField(c1)).toBe(textoOriginal);
  });

  it('deve utilizar HMAC-SHA256 alimentado por chave secreta para índice de unicidade', () => {
    process.env.HMAC_PEPPER_KEY = pepper;
    const email = 'carlos.silva@universidade.edu.br';
    const hash = getEmailHash(email);
    expect(hash).toHaveLength(64);
    expect(hash).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);
    expect(hash).toBe(getEmailHash('  CARLOS.SILVA@UNIVERSIDADE.EDU.BR  '));
  });
});

describe('8.3 Auditoria de Segregação de Cofre e Pseudonimização', () => {
  it('deve segregar PII no cofre e pseudonimizar registros operacionais no bucket', () => {
    process.env.VAULT_ENCRYPTION_KEY = 'chave_teste_vault_secret_32bytes_!';
    process.env.HMAC_PEPPER_KEY = 'pepper_teste_hmac_secret_key_2026';
    const raw = {
      nome: 'Prof. Carlos', email: 'carlos@ies.edu.br', telefone: '(11) 98765-4321',
      instituicao: 'Universidade X', campus: 'Central', cargo: 'Coordenador',
      curso: 'Adm', previsaoSemestre: '1/2027', observacoes: 'Dados sigilosos',
    };
    const docId = 'lead_uuid_123';
    const vault = buildVaultRecord(raw, docId);
    const hash = getEmailHash(raw.email);
    const op = buildOperationalRecord(raw, docId, hash);

    expect(vault.nome).toMatch(/^v1:/);
    expect(vault.email).toMatch(/^v1:/);
    expect(vault.telefone).toMatch(/^v1:/);
    expect(vault.observacoes).toMatch(/^v1:/);
    expect(op).not.toHaveProperty('nome');
    expect(op).not.toHaveProperty('email');
    expect(op).not.toHaveProperty('telefone');
    expect(op).not.toHaveProperty('observacoes');
    expect(op.temObservacoes).toBe(true);
    expect(op.emailHash).toBe(hash);
    expect(op.vaultRef).toBe('vault_leads/lead_uuid_123');
  });
});
